<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * in the gutter.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Icons: when a list (or a section's rows) holds a row or group label with an `icon`, the rows</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * and labels beside it that have none move in by the icon's width, so every text starts on one</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * edge. That happens on its own; set `--sidebar-nav-inset` on a wrapper to force or cancel it.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *   &#x3C;SidebarNav></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;SidebarNavSearch shortcut="⌘K" onClick={openSearch} /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;SidebarNavContent aria-label="Docs"></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *       &#x3C;SidebarNavSection label="Guides" count={3}></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *         &#x3C;SidebarNavList></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *           &#x3C;SidebarNavLink href="/docs" active>Introduction&#x3C;/SidebarNavLink></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *         &#x3C;/SidebarNavList></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *         &#x3C;SidebarNavSection level="group" label="Forms" href="/docs/forms">…&#x3C;/SidebarNavSection></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *       &#x3C;/SidebarNavSection></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;/SidebarNavContent></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;SidebarNavFooter>…&#x3C;/SidebarNavFooter></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *   &#x3C;/SidebarNav></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * The icon-aligned inset. A container whose direct rows or group labels carry an icon sets</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * `--sidebar-nav-inset` to the icon slot plus its gap (16px + 8px); rows and labels without an</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * icon add it to their start padding. Nested groups inherit it, so their rows line up too.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> iconInsetScope</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">  "[&#x26;:has(>[data-slot=sidebar-nav-list]>[data-slot=sidebar-nav-item]>*>[data-slot=sidebar-nav-link-icon])]:[--sidebar-nav-inset:1.5rem] [&#x26;:has(>[data-slot=sidebar-nav-section]>[data-slot=sidebar-nav-section-label]_[data-slot=sidebar-nav-section-icon])]:[--sidebar-nav-inset:1.5rem]"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> insetStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "ps-[calc(0.5rem+var(--sidebar-nav-inset,0px))] pe-2"</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * The scrolling list of sections. On mount it scrolls the current row (`aria-current="page"`)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * into view when it would start below the fold.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> SidebarNavContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"nav"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">HTMLElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useEffect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> el</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> box?.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">querySelector</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">HTMLElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">'[aria-current="page"]'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Renders the label link with your router's link, such as `&#x3C;Link href="…" />`. Implies a link label. */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Rows of a section or group; no indent, so every label and row shares one left edge. When one</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * row has an icon, the rows without one start at its text (see `--sidebar-nav-inset`).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> SidebarNavList</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"ul"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">ul</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> data-slot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"sidebar-nav-list"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">cn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"flex flex-col gap-px"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, iconInsetScope, className)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> SidebarNavLinkProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> useRender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"a"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** The current page: a fill, a bar in the gutter and `aria-current="page"`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  active</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Trailing content, such as a "New" badge. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  badge</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Leading 16px icon, muted until the row is current. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
